# go role move ledger

The owned moves and repository-wide frozen-path import integration are implemented
in the shared `0xcadams/role-organization` worktree, based on `bc1efd09` (domain
PR #448). The compile-only integration pass reaches every root-module package;
two extraction mismatches remain below. Generation, formatting, and behavioral
test execution remain with the parent. The original move inventory follows the
latest integration record.

## frozen-path integration follow-up

The central sweep updated 462 source files, including commands, all role owners,
public Go consumers, Python native helpers, negative compiler source strings,
and generator inputs. Moved package names keep explicit aliases where needed.
Four callers importing both old app owners now use one `publisher/app` import
and one qualifier. No forwarding package, public type wrapper, or generated Go
edit was introduced.

- All original internal import roots now resolve to the final maps in this ledger
  and the publisher/connector ledgers. A read-only audit including Task, contract,
  and SDK source/configuration files reports zero stale old-owner path literals;
  historical documentation and generated provenance are excluded from that claim.
- `oidcauth.Prompt` consumers now use `clientauth.Prompt` from `client/auth`.
  `pkg/tnl.Publisher` remains a direct alias of `publisher/identity.Native`.
- `internal/config/generate.go` selects `scripts/generate/config-types.ts` and
  `internal/client/project/keys.gen.json`; the config generator writes that same
  key-mapping destination. Source templates and negative compiler programs follow
  the actual import owners. Generated output is left for its owning generator.
- Removed command selection/profile helpers now call `connector.Select` and
  `connector.SelectService`. The group namespace-renaming network proof uses the
  selected service's exact ID and managed hostname, as required by the connector
  ledger; profile construction remains connector-owned.
- The development helpers moved in full: `internal/devdns/**` to
  `internal/development/dns/**`, and `internal/devoidc/**` to
  `internal/development/oidc/**`. All five files moved, including their two test
  files. Their consumer paths and the OIDC fixture's repository-root depth follow
  the new locations.
- Both binary ClientHello fixtures moved byte-for-byte using Git moves, with the
  index restored to an unstaged state afterward. Blob IDs are
  `7b62cc6914ef4e27fa9e5313c041b4c299c21081` (Chromium) and
  `d39958259502226cc73180334ce5aabdb77aac94` (Firefox). All obsolete internal
  owner directories and their empty descendants were removed using empty-only
  directory removal. No old owner has remaining files or an empty directory.
- No commits, staged changes, global generation, or formatting were produced.

`github.com/tnldotdev/tnl/internal/connector`,
`github.com/tnldotdev/tnl/internal/publisher/invocation`,
`github.com/tnldotdev/tnl/internal/publisher/local`, and
`github.com/tnldotdev/tnl/cmd/tnl`. The last two are blocked by the publisher
operation dependency; their own remaining type errors, if any, cannot yet be
qualified. The publisher and connector owners should repair the two boundaries,
then repeat the same compile-only Task before the parent's generation/format and
behavioral validation sequence.

## initial move execution boundary

- `/Users/chase/.worktree/tnl/role-organization`; no commit, staging, push, generator run, formatter run, or test run.
- All 1053 baseline files are now relocated; the follow-up above closes the two
  binary fixture moves that were pending in the initial text-only snapshot.
- 479 existing test files preserved, with 1206 named
  Test/Fuzz/Benchmark entrypoints and 9597 textual testing failure sites
  (`Fatal`, `Fatalf`, `Error`, `Errorf`, `Fail`, `FailNow`). The service-audit
  file split adds one destination test file without adding or removing a test.
- The source comparison accounts for all 1206 original named entrypoints after
  owned import-path, fixture-depth, issuer-alias, target-snapshot qualification,
  and maintenance-call renames. Bodies match except the separately inventoried
  target-TLS check extraction below. Failure-site counts match per original file,
  including fixture helpers.
  The publisher agent split the target-TLS rejection check from a wire test
  into one additional named test without changing the combined failure-site
  count; that extracted assertion is inventoried separately below.
- 68 committed generated Go files, 63 SQL source files, and
  7 remaining non-Go assets/configurations match the baseline byte-for-byte.
  The project TypeScript configuration has only its required depth correction.
- Existing implementation package names are retained except the extracted
  `maintenance` package. Intra-owned imports follow the destinations below;
  imports from other agents' owners are left for global integration.

children, HTTP mechanics, `serviceapi`, `workerloop`, and public `pkg/api/*`,
`pkg/tnl`, and `pkg/protocol/tunnelv1` retain their ownership. Publisher-family
and connector-family implementation moves, commands/app/local extraction,
OpenAPI/tooling/Task/SDK changes, external fixtures, and root guidance belong to
their assigned agents or the parent integrator.

The assets agent's follow-on fixture move places the daemon's former local
testdata under `tests/integration/fixtures/{compose-publisher,dns,separated-stack}`.
The exact inventory below records these destinations. Owned Go test references
now follow that fixture layout and `development/local/oidc-admission.json`.

## boundary and file splits

All state splits keep the existing `controlstate` package. Whole declarations
move without modifying transaction bodies, lock scopes, or rollback ordering.
The split audit recovered every original source-body byte before writing the
parts; only per-file import lists changed.

| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `oidcauth/config.go:Prompt`                                                                                                                   | `client/auth/prompt.go`; the client owns the approval prompt DTO, and client auth no longer imports control OIDC                                                                                                                   |
| `visitorcert/issuer.go`                                                                                                                       | issuer and CSR validation in `control/certificates/visitor/issuer.go`; lifetime bounds and the one sentinel in `security/visitorcert/bounds.go`                                                                                    |
| `controlstate/public_urls.go`                                                                                                                 | `control/state/public_urls.go`, `public_url_updates.go`, `public_url_expiration.go`, `public_url_lookup.go`, `public_url_deletion.go`, `publish_run_closure.go`, `public_url_validation.go`, `public_url_projection.go`            |

The visitor issuer imports neither state nor certificate workers. Client state
uses only the shared bounds/sentinel in product code; its certificate tests use
the independent issuer directly. No forwarding facades or duplicate sentinel
values were added. Worker intervals, operation timeouts, batch sizes, shutdown
lifetimes, error text, and metric labels are retained.

## test discovery and remaining integration

- Parent updates package lists/selectors/shards from the recursive map. Routine
  tests remain under `task go:test`; state/runtime integration and load tests keep
  their existing `-tnl-*` guards and their existing opt-in Task tiers.
- Add `internal/control/maintenance` to the existing focused cleanup/server/
  observability coverage selectors that previously selected daemon-only tests.
  The five moved routine test entrypoints are listed with their destinations
  below; real database/topology cleanup proofs stay in `internal/daemon`.
- State/runtime integration harness package paths become
  `internal/control/state` and `internal/daemon`. Preserve state/runtime shard
  membership and the dedicated real-clock lease/workload selectors.
- Global imports are pending outside owned files, including retained config,
  ingress, relay, HTTP mechanics, publisher, connector, commands, benchmark, and
  the separate `tools/testharness` module. This tree is not claimed to compile yet.
- Prompt consumers must replace `oidcauth.Prompt` with `clientauth.Prompt`
  imported from `internal/client/auth`; publisher identity proof and target/app/
  local/command extraction continue under their assigned owners.
- Tooling agent must close project loader/config-key and diagnostic generator
  mappings, Go generator ordering, generated-path/coverage/formatter lists,
  Taskfiles, CI, scripts, deploy paths, and external fixture selectors. The
  OpenAPI/SDK agents retain their own source and build mappings.
- Binary path handoff is complete; see the matching blob IDs in the integration
  follow-up. The initial pending import/selector notes in this section describe
  the pre-integration snapshot; the latest follow-up is authoritative.
- No other assigned path is omitted. Required parent checks remain the plan's
  generation, formatting, Go routine/lint/race/harness/integration/build, SDK,
  Python, action, workflow, and packaging checks on the globally integrated tree.
  No tested commit or pass result exists for this uncommitted move snapshot.

## preserved test-file and entrypoint inventory

Each named entrypoint retains its existing invariant and test body; the source
comparison permits only the mechanical renames described above. Failure sites
include helper files, so a zero-entrypoint fixture still has explicit inventory.
Tier labels identify existing source-family discovery; individual test guards
remain authoritative, and parent Task discovery still needs execution.
